- [Byline/date; How we built LangGraph for human-in-the-loop workflows; interrupt: a new developer experience; Common human-in-the-loop workflows](https://www.langchain.com/blog/making-it-easier-to-build-human-in-the-loop-agents-with-interrupt)：The LangChain Team published this official article on 14 December 2024. Checkpoints preserve graph state; execution can pause and resume after human edits. The interrupt function collects external input and resumes with Command(resume=...). Documented patterns include approve/reject, edit state, review tool calls, and multi-turn human input in a multi-agent setup.；获取范围：official_full_text。

处理建议：Retain as an independent dated official HITL source. Keep the January multi-agent workflow source for graph topology. It supports mechanisms, not empirical QA safety or quality guarantees.
